Goal: replace SQLAlchemy with Tortoise ORM across the Mealie backend.

Fixed decisions:
- Use Tortoise ORM, which is async. When the rewrite is finished, nothing under mealie/
  should import SQLAlchemy.
- Keep support for both SQLite and PostgreSQL.
- Replace Alembic with Aerich. Upgrading existing databases from the old Alembic history
  is out of scope.
- Keep the HTTP API and the Pydantic schemas in mealie/schema unchanged, so the frontend
  does not need to change.
- Database access becomes async. Make routes, services and scheduler tasks async where
  they need to be.
- You may change how the tests set up and access the database (tests/conftest.py,
  tests/fixtures/, and database setup code inside test files). You must not delete tests,
  skip them, mark them xfail, remove or weaken assertions, or change expected values.
- Run the tests with: uv run pytest -n 4 --dist loadfile
  (tests in the same file depend on each other, so they must run grouped by file).
- Do not push, and do not change git remotes or git configuration.

Read AGENTS.md first. Step C1 did not pass the gate: 3217 passed, 1 failed, 0 errors, 18 skipped; mypy 0 errors; test functions 1180; asserts 3914; skip markers 12. Fix this before doing anything else, then update the progress table.

DatetimeFieldQueryValueType | None, instance: type[Model] | Model\n    ) -> DatetimeFieldQueryValueType | None:\n        if isinstance(value, datetime):\n            value = value.replace(tzinfo=UTC) if value.tzinfo is None else value.astimezone(UTC)\n\n        converted = super().to_db_value(value, instance)\n        if isinstance(converted, datetime):\n            return converted.astimezone(UTC).replace(tzinfo=None)\n        return converted\n\n    def to_python_value(self, value: Any) -> datetime | None:\n        converted = super().to_python_value(value)\n        if converted is None:\n            return None\n        if converted.tzinfo is None:\n            return converted.replace(tzinfo=UTC)\n        return converted.astimezone(UTC)\n\n\nclass TortoiseBase(Model):\n    id: fields.IntField[int] = fields.IntField(primary_key=True)\n    created_at: fields.DatetimeField[datetime | None] = UTCDateTimeField(auto_now_add=True, null=True, db_index=True)\n    update_at: fields.DatetimeField[datetime | None] = UTCDateTimeField(auto_now=True, null=True)\n\n    class Meta:\n        abstract = True\n\n    @property\n    def updated_at(self) -> datetime | None:\n        return self.update_at\n\n    @updated_at.setter\n    def updated_at(self, value: datetime | None) -> None:\n        self.update_at = value\n\n    @classmethod\n    def normalize(cls, value: str) -> str:\n        return unidecode(value).translate(_NORMALIZE_PUNCTUATION_TABLE).lower().strip()[:255]\n",

## agent_message at 2026-10-11T05:10:12.231288+00:00

The C1 failure comes from startup’s health query: Tortoise wraps SQLite’s connection error in a different exception type. I’ll open the connection before running the query, preserving the expected startup error and cleanup behavior.

I also found that concurrent diagnostic tests disrupted the first run’s temporary databases. The final suite will run on its own.
